# Tensor24: the architecture

The one document that tells the system and how it is built, and the one the work runs from (the owner's words of 2026-10-30, in Hebrew, in translation: "a single document, with the decoder in it, no more splitting documents"; "not bloated"; "clear and clean and orderly"). The law ([docs/ALGEBRA.md](ALGEBRA.md)) governs it and the owner decides it. The words it uses are in the glossary at the end.

Tensor24 is a purely algebraic engine: a board of Node-clocks, each a set of gears that mesh with its six neighbours' gears through six Meshes, and one act at every Node-clock, alike everywhere, which the runner repeats in rounds. Rule3's carry is its one division, with the remainder kept. Nothing of nature is typed; the physics is read from the clicks alone.

**Each part answers the same questions**, a few lines each, and one general flag, n/a, stands where a layer does not apply:

- **Clock:** what the clock actually does.
- **Algebra:** one line of its algebra, in whole numbers and Rule3's carry.
- **Board:** what the board does in terms of the clocks, the board taken as it stands in our event space, which has no time: time is each Node-clock's own count and nothing else (a round is the runner's pass and a gear's now and before are its two counts; none of them is a time of the board's).
- **Clicks:** what physics shows in the clicks, the board's shadow; the physics stands behind the clicks, and the step from the board to the clicks is the delicate one.
- Then its **home** in the code, its **test** and its **status**.

## Solution24: how every question is solved

**Clocks are the board; physics is the click world** (the owner's words to Big Pic, directly, relayed at 00:42 UTC in his 6536666441, in Hebrew, in his translation, the verb of "arrange" plural, to all: "It must be in the click world, but clock fixes are indeed in the board world, pay attention ... Arrange Solution24 accordingly ... Physics is only the click world. Clocks are the board."; line 35, item 18). Two worlds and two checks: a **clock fix**, a change to a clock, a gear, a dial, a Mesh or the act, lives on the board and is checked there, in whole numbers, with no root and no if, its locality, the carry's sum, the run back exact, and its probe with the mutant that must fail; a **claim of physics** lives in the click world and is checked there, twice, by the decoder's tools. A board check never stands for physics, and a click-world reading never stands for a clock fix. Each line of a text names its world.

Solution24 is our one method of solving, for a term, a part, a reading or a change of code: the owner's principle of the clock and the gear (law line 35) with solving from the big things (line 106), as the law has them, under the name he gave it (his word of 2026-10-30, 13:0x UTC). Steps 3 and 5 to 8 carry the additions of David (four, 13:2x UTC), the Boss (two, 13:3x) and Big Pic (two, 13:3x), each a lesson of its author's own readers or probes; Big Pic's two stand with the unit piece as DRAFTED until both its readers pass it. Steps 6 to 9 carry five rules and one change of the readers' (the owner's "Yes, I agree" of 15:50 UTC, relayed in the Boss's 6527026433), each an instance of the law that closes the steps, **Before a finding is stated**. Whenever there is a problem, in any part, it is solved by Solution24 (the owner's word of 13:1x UTC: "Always use it when there is a problem"), and every derivation, plan section and push says whether it addressed it.

1. **Does it follow by itself?** Ask first whether it follows from the one act of the Node-clock on the board of clocks. If it does, nothing is added. If it does not, ask the clicks what shows that it is needed: what does not follow is named, never declared (Postulate 6 was the first case).
2. **Take the picture.** A board of clocks, each a train of gears, the gears of neighbouring clocks meshing through their Meshes. Time belongs to the clock (its count, its beat, the order of its reads) and arithmetic to the gear (every number a gear's notch). The board is taken as it stands in our event space, which has no time.
3. **Build from the big things only.** The families (the kinds), the tensor, the 24, the clocks (gears, Rule3's carry, the dial) and time's reversal, in whole numbers and Gaussian integers. A floor, a bound, a clamp, a mask, an if, a table, a declaration, a closed form of a result, a number typed into an act or a rule written for a phenomenon is a fake and goes to the owner. Only generic laws are approved: a law stands only if the clocks explain it and the tensors and the 24 realize it (lines 106, 96). A strength the law leaves free is CHOSEN, with its reason and its measured window, and never written DERIVED.
4. **Tell it in four layers**, starting from the simplest for the question and closing all four: the clock (which clock or gear makes it), the algebra (the act, Rule3's carry, the kinds and their tables), the event board (what stands and moves at each Node-clock, Mesh and round) and the physics behind the clicks (what the clicks read; the step from the board to the clicks is the delicate one). The clock, the algebra and the event board are the board's layers; the physics behind the clicks is the click world's, and each line of a text names its world. Forward (clock, algebra, board, physics) or backward (physics, board, algebra, clock); n/a where a layer does not apply (line 35, item 10).
5. **Count the click.** A click is the carry's quotient where a winding is whole; three kinds: the whole, where the wind decides; the split, where three clocks meet at one place and the passing splits by the structure over the triangle's three edges (the Boss's ruling of 04:2x UTC), with no choice built in src; and the third, the click that acts, the triangle's own tooth landing one circulation (entered in the law on the owner's word of 2026-10-31, 00:34 UTC, "Yes, as a third kind already now", and stated in the law of the three clicks; its choice is the path, its form THIRD7's, run on Big Pic's bench as mode 11 and NOT PASSED by its pair, THIRD8 folding the readers' items); its form, a change of the act, is checked on the board, and its weights and the past and the future it changes are physics, read in the click world. The beat and the time gear's turn are no clicks (line 35, items 4 and 5). Nature reads one quantum: a mass, a rate or a probability is read per quantum, and a swing of many quanta reads their interaction. In the clicks, think clocks and not quanta: a click shows clocks connecting and coming apart, a hand of one meshing with a hand of another, and the system's aim is that clocks connect (the owner's word, relayed at 15:05 UTC in the Boss's 6525992153, in translation: "In the clicks, think clocks; do not think quantum. Clicks are to be thought in clocks, hands, chains, all those things. That is how to solve it: think of everything as clicks. The whole system as clicks: clocks connect and come apart, and the aim of the system is that clocks connect. That is what clicks are for: to show that clocks connect, right?"); what a click counts is the whole windings of line 35 (4), seen as a connection of clocks (the Boss's and Big Pic's reading, not derived: the whole turns passed from one hand to another, each sense apart).
6. **Check the conservation laws in the clicks.** The carry keeps its sum exactly, the edges' consistency check of every click holds (the books, by construction) and the run back is exact to the bit; flux on the board, energy, momentum and charge follow from the symmetries; a conservation kept on the board and lost in the clicks is no solution (line 35, item 6). Energy and momentum are no invariant of the board: they are conserved in the clicks' spacetime, so a check of them first derives the passage from our event space to that spacetime and reads them there (the owner's word, relayed at 14:50 UTC in the Boss's 6525676674, in translation: "when you check these things, it must be derived: it must pass from our event space to the spacetime in which the clicks are, where momentum and energy are conserved"). Time's reversal is checked as the act itself on the mirrored state, every read (a draw, a record, a source) placed where the backward act reads it the same; a recomputation outside the act checks nothing. Ask each quantity what the 24 and the mirrored state (time's reversal) make of it: a click counts only what the 24 keep (A1 through the tables) and the mirror sends to its opposite, a turn; a count the mirror keeps is never a click's count. A theorem's hypotheses are measured by cases built to pass the stated rule and still break, not only by cases that break the rule: a case that passes the stated hypotheses and still breaks the conclusion shows a hypothesis missing.
7. **Go both ways.** Forward from the clocks (gears, whole numbers, the one act) and backward from the clicks: the physics of nature seen in them is the direction and the test; what does not match nature is named, never hidden (line 35, item 7). Read nature only in pure numbers: a count, a tooth or a key is a setting of the board, and the clicks read only its pure combinations (each strength times the key, a laid area over the key, a ratio of beats); name nature's numbers with their sources and bounds. Name where it holds: every result states where it is exact and where it is OPEN, and where a check runs (one Node-clock or a board with Meshes; one lay or many): a count that moves with the lay is read over many lays with its null spread, and a result is "consistent with" at the tests run, never "bounds". A strength or a sign is measured on the slots the act pushes, never on slots laid by hand. Every whole-number measure states the stride it runs at: below one notch the act is not its continuum. A claimed mean is read by windows over the run, and "bounded" or "settles" names its width in time, never one sum or a few samples.
8. **Run it locally.** Local runs are excellent: take the Node-clock and crack it locally, or run it and check it stage by stage, in a probe in whole numbers on the real act or in a draft of the code in the code writer's own worktree. Anything may be tried there; nothing of a draft reaches main before it is DERIVED, and a draft on a branch is for running and reading (line 35, item 12). Before the readers read a derivation, every claim in it is checked by such a probe on the engine's own act, yes and no: the probe has its "no" case, a mutant it must catch; the probe stays local, and main's tests stay one small test per part (line 106). A cut of tests reruns every case the old tests held, each reader's mutant among them, against the new tests before the push, and names each case no longer caught: kept as a plain assertion, ruled equivalent, or dropped on the Boss's word. A deriver always checks himself before he reports, as a hostile reader would: he reruns his probes, re-derives each step and tries to break his own result by cases built to pass his stated rules, and his report names what he checked and how (the owner's word, relayed at 16:00 UTC in the Boss's 6527238774, in translation: "A deriver always checks himself.").
9. **Two hostile readers.** Two hands derive blind before any code or document, and every derivation, the Boss's agents' included, passes a hostile mathematician and a hostile physicist, each new to it at the first reading. A text that a pair refuses goes back to the same pair, for the blocking items only, and the pair keeps its notes; a new pair reads it only at the end, before DERIVED, so that "each new to it" stands for that last pair (the owner's "Yes, I agree" of 15:50 UTC, relayed in the Boss's 6527026433). Only then is it DERIVED, entered here by the writer, and only then does the Boss brief the code writer for main (Postulate 10; line 35, item 11).
10. **Unify.** At every step look for several operations that are one; the one is written once and the others are its cases (line 104).
11. **Put it in the document** the moment it is ready, in the four layers and with its status: no code starts on what is not READY, and main takes a change only when its derivation stands here and its candidate is approved (section 4).
12. **Say whether the method was addressed:** which clock or gear makes each term and how its fix was checked on the board, what the clicks show (the books first), as read in the click world, twice, or with the word that no click-world reading exists, and what does not follow, asked of the clicks (line 35, items 8 and 16).
13. **Go bigger.** When Solution24 reaches no solution, take the problem one level up and ask whether a law above Tensor24 is wrong or incomplete for this case, a postulate or a rule of docs/ALGEBRA.md: name the law, the case where it fails and the whole-number probe that would decide it, and bring it first to Big Pic, who looks at everything from above (through the Boss, Big Pic working with him alone). A piece that its readers refuse twice on a matter of substance (a claim, a law or a design; a minor item or a wording goes straight back to its writer or deriver, line 35 item 19), or that waits on a law, goes to Big Pic at once, and Big Pic answers in a few words: where the solution lies and whether a law above is the problem. Always think of the Node-clock going forward and also going backward: the solution for the quanta, for all things, may come from there, when one goes up to solve (the owner's word, relayed at 14:59 UTC in the Boss's 6525868155, in translation: "always think also of the Node-clock going forward, and the Node-clock also going backward. Maybe the solution for the quanta comes from there, for all things. It comes when you go up and try to solve"); the law's line 1021, **The click is the meeting of the future with the past**, and line 35's "the present is where the two runs meet at the Node-clock" stand beside it. A law changes only on the owner's word: the proposal waits in the law's Laws with a question mark (line 106), the law stands as written, and what waits on it stays OPEN (line 35, item 13).
14. **The aim and the end.** The board builds ever more complex clocks by its clicks and carries alone, nothing declared (line 35, item 9); the system is closed once the Node-clock works well (the owner's word of 12:4x UTC).

**Before a finding is stated.** One law over every step above (line 35, item 15; the owner's word, relayed at 15:47 UTC in the Boss's 6526950092, in translation: "Put into the writer that whenever someone finds something, break these steps down into what you found and think about it again, so that people do not just say things. Let it be a law with the writer."). Whoever finds something, a deriver, a reader, Big Pic, David, the Boss or the writer, takes the finding through the steps one by one, each thought through again against what was found, and only then states it as found; a finding that has not been through the steps is not said as found. It holds for every kind of finding: a derivation's result, a reader's refusal (which Big Pic reads again, How we work), a probe's measure, a reading of a push, a claim about the law or the code. Step 12 already asks a text to say what it addressed, and the writer holds his own readings to the law: what he finds in a push he takes through the steps before he says it.

**Where a claim of physics is checked.** One law over every claim (line 35, item 16; the owner's words of 23:46 and 23:51 UTC, relayed in the Boss's 6535922454 and 6536002736, whole in SCRIBE-OWNER-WORDS.md: everything is looked at on the level of the click world, dimension three, the board's shadow, converted with the tools we know and checked there; a basic decoder on the clicks that shows the physics there; a ban, and a check twice on everyone, the writer included). Every claim of physics (light, gravity, the clock's beat, gamma, the books and every measured number that is repeated) is transformed to the click world and checked there. **The ban:** no claim of physics stands on a board reading alone, in any text, post, document or report; a world with no click line says so by name, not in the click world, and shows nothing. **The double check:** every claim of physics is checked twice in the click world, by two independent readings among the tools known for the shadow, the decoder's readings and the measurement's rules (section 3, readings 1 and 3 to 6); no new tool is made for one claim; it binds everyone, the writer included; a text says in step 12 what the clicks show as read in the click world, twice, or says that it has none. **The words of a measure:** every line of this document that says measured is MEASURED on the board, a measure of the act and no claim of physics; it becomes MEASURED in the click world, a claim of physics, only where two readings in the click world stand and are named; no status moves by the law alone (a pair's pass moves a status, and its physicist reads the click-world readings as part of the physics).

**The board has no light and no matter.** One rule over every text (line 35, item 16; the owner's words of 01:44 UTC, relayed in the Boss's 6537340494, whole in SCRIBE-OWNER-WORDS.md: when a derivation talks physics it is in the click world, and in the click world light is seen; there is no real light on the board). The board's wave of the A1 train, with its speed and its cone, is a clock fact of the board: this document names it the board's wave and never light, and where it keeps the files' name for the train it says so. Light is only what the click world shows, light's clicks and its echo between clocks; no world writes a light click line that a reader has passed (NOT_CLOSED 4), so every sentence about light's speed, its cone, its bending or its delay is a claim of physics owed to the click world, to be read there twice. A derivation or a reader's page that speaks of light on the board is read against this rule, and the keeper's read of a candidate looks for it in the diff's docstrings, comments and messages.

**Light and matter only in the click world** (the owner widened it at 03:21 UTC, the Boss's 6538346347, whole in SCRIBE-OWNER-WORDS.md): no board-side name, docstring, comment, test name, README line or derivation sentence says light or matter; a board thing, whether a train, a row, a hand, a field or a source of the push, is named as a clock's; light and matter are what the decoder shows in the click record, light's clicks, its echo between clocks, and the clocks that click; this document says the board's wave and the clocks' rows where it once said light and matter on the board, and keeps light and matter where it reads the click record (the decoder, the clicks' readings, the files' names for a train, quoted words). **Physics only in the click world** (the owner's words of 02:08 UTC, relayed in the Boss's 6537595690, whole in SCRIBE-OWNER-WORDS.md): the physicist derivers and readers look at the physics only in the click world, by Big Pic's methods, and at the clocks in the board world; a claim of physics is a reading of the click record (the meetings, their order, each clock's own count of its events, light's clicks and its echo between clocks); a board reading is a clock fact, never physics, whatever its numbers.

**The law of the choice** (its own line of the law, the owner's word of 03:37 UTC through the Boss's 6538500636 and 6538507053): there is a choice for the third click, for every clock inside the universe, and for the universe as a whole there is none, the run back being exact. **The law of the path** (its own line, the owner's word of 05:56 UTC directly to the keeper, in the Boss's wording of 6539844817 as Big Pic amended it in 6539934782): the board is deterministic and the path of the clicks is not; from the end state alone the act runs back to the start exactly, board and teeth; two runs alike in every count of the board and one hidden unit apart in one tooth write different clicks, only at that tooth's clock, and reach one end board; the first click, with no draw, reaches the same end board; the difference lives at the third click.

**The law of the three clicks** (the law's last line, the owner's word to Big Pic of 05:5x UTC, in the text the Boss gave in his 6540150830 on Big Pic's 6539991906 item 2 with David's wording): at every Node-clock whose edges each lie on at most one triangle, the first click is each edge's own share of the passing on its one pair of teeth with no draw, the split click the passing split by the structure with no division and no condition, the third click each triangle's own tooth and its hidden twin carrying the triangle's circulating passing, so that every clock's line is made by its own edges' carries of their shares alone, the same whatever path the third click takes, and over any run it books its own passing within its teeth. An edge on two triangles or more is OPEN with two roads (the law's own split at the dial 3D, proved on every structure and read on two toy structures, and the split of n clocks all pairwise joined, B^T B + C C^T = n I, at the dial n D), the sentence staying OPEN until Big Pic's extended proof passes its pair; the proof is his L0 to L9 (scratch three_clicks/PICTURE.md section 7 at bigpic-3clicks 0675423a3 and after), L0 to L3, L5 and L6, and L9 holding in whole numbers and L7 and L8 resting on the engine's mirror and the carry's sign law. The texts' verdicts and the memory of the landings stand in "The third click, its state" and in the register.

**The derivation of every part** (the owner's word of 06:2x UTC through the Boss's 6539961086, whole in SCRIBE-OWNER-WORDS.md): every part of the architecture carries its derivation in three views, how it is explained in the clock, in the algebra and in the click world. The architect's map (boss-derive night_1031/ARCH_MAP.md, 1,039 lines) gives every part its derivation texts with each one's status, its three views or OWED where no text gives one, and every line of this document that was out of date (list K, 64 lines, and list L, the parts built and not named); this document is edited by it and says which views are owed. The hostile audit (boss-derive night_1031/CLEAN.md): the engine is clean on every head (no loop, branch, comparison or mask in engine/ or algebra/, no number beyond 0 and 1, // and % only in algebra/carry.py, no INDICATOR in an act); about 25 items of old code and about 55 old test cases go to the fold writer; its section F is the keeper's documents. **The map's count:** 68 parts (22 modules, terms 1 to 13, the three clicks, the initialization, the record, 22 decoder readings, the run back, the seed test, the 24 and the runaway's step), 49 holding all three views and 19 owing one (18 owe only the click-world view; readings/__init__.py, an empty package, owes all three).

**The click-world views owed** (18; Big Pic's lines, his 6540247895, from PICTURE.md's readings): six given in part by the first click's books edge by edge, never yet as light or mass (mesh.py and the pull; contract.py, plans.py and term 6; the key, each click's quantum, an edge booking one click per D = 3 key of its passing and the triangle's tooth one per 3D of its circulating passing); one with no click of its own, stated (the notch: it joins no two clocks and lays no edge); eleven OWED with what is missing named: the doublets (a click names a train, never a row, so they need three species' own clocks in a world); term 2's proper time (a clock's own count is its own lines in the record's order, the Boss's ruling of 6540720252: nothing new is needed and FACE2's field is not built; the reading itself is owed); term 3's lengths and term 4's light (light's echo: LIGHT2's verdicts stand in the register); term 5's rates (a clock's own count and the echo rule); term 10 (not built as a term, so in no record); term 13 (two clicking clocks at two depths, each with its own count); row 46 (its reader cut, waiting on a world that lays clocks of clocks); mass (not derivable now, Big Pic's 6540755041: no shipped world shows the light links that bound a beat, the strengths that set every beat are chosen in the files, and heavy bound matter runs away above a size; the owner's picture of the least masses in the generations, the electron and the proton, is OPEN: the lightest of each design ends in nothing lighter and is seen alone, as the first click is, and the proton's three members pairwise bound have a triangle's shape, where the split click and the third click live; MASS1 starts on the owner's word of about 08:00 UTC); the 24's isotropy (a one-record reading of the meetings' directions over the three axes, which needs light's lines; the 24 hold on lines and sides, check24, 24 of 24); and the runaway's step (the confinement is the board's own, since a click writes nothing into the gears, its candidate STOP1's stop; a runaway shows in the click world only as the record that is not written).

**Eight texts stand open** (the owner's words to the Boss of about 06:50 UTC, relayed in the Boss's 6540451346, of about 07:30 UTC, in his 6540720252, of about 08:00 UTC, in his 6540917395, and of about 08:15 UTC, in his 6541083468, all whole in SCRIBE-OWNER-WORDS.md: that the node is truly minimal in its gear teeth and springs and nothing is doubled, what the great mathematics of the whole closed system is, that there are no fakes and the runner synchronizes everything as it should, short checks that the click world shows what nature shows, the worlds seen running, from the minimality check which new algebra is more correct and orders the world of clocks better, and, from the base masses and the uncertainty principle, what the click world says of them, and that a fourth click is off the table), each routed to a hostile pair before anything is built; their verdicts stand in The proofs by name. MIN1: every state element and spring of one Node-clock with the act that reads it and the law that needs it. MATH1: the whole closed system as one map with each part's standing. ALG1: the clocks' coupling complex (every coupling a cell joining its clocks, the teeth as cochains, the books as d d = 0); Big Pic's check (his 6540828583, PICTURE.md section 10): it closes the books' side and not the tie's residual, which no cell can carry and which closes only by a step that makes the quartic's work an exact difference (the miracle's two roads), bounded once STOP1 holds the square-2 lay; its twin goes on the dial D alone (THIRD8's mathematician, in the third click's state) and a 3-cell holds no tooth, its quanta never reaching a side or a line (C d_3 = 0) and its boundary being THIRD7's kept direction, so that the pairs sit on C_2 / Z_2 and that double goes; a record of lines and sides then shows three kinds of click by the cell's dimension, edges, triangles and the split between them, and no fourth (the owner's word of about 08:15 UTC, "Big Pic said something about a fourth click; sync with him and make sure it is off the table", and the Boss's ruling of 6541083468: three clicks, no fourth, a 3-cell gets no tooth and no twin); ALG1's pair starts with the fourth click as its first question, and Big Pic confirmed it on his bench (his 6541185833 and bigpic-3clicks 04225df85, fourth.py: no shipped world has four clocks pairwise joined, no live term joins more than two trains, and on the four-clock toy the 3-cell's boundary books 0 on all six edges and on all four clocks' lines, so no lay the files allow writes a fourth kind of event into the lines and sides), PICTURE.md section 10 now naming the 3-cell closed by ALG1's derivation until its pair rules. AUDIT2: the converged code read for every fake that section 4 names. NATURE1: every phenomenon of nature that a click reading can show, each with a check that can fail, or OWED with what it waits on. VIEW1: a page drawn from a run's record, the board and the click world with the decoder's verdicts beside them, no light or matter on the board (the visualizer's text, OWED). MASS1: the base masses in the click world; the measured ratio, about 1,836.15, is a number a lawful reading must meet, never a target to fit and never a reason to pick a strength or a key. UNC1: the uncertainty principle read in the click world from click lines alone, with its no, worked with the teeth in the record as node-all writes them and without them. The Boss's answer to the owner's question whether the families are in the code (6540720252): the 24's five kinds are in the code; the half-speed families (the 48 lifts, the two half-speed trains, the three doublets on the three axes) are on node-all, no shipped world lays them, the loader refuses a half-speed train from a world's file (Big Pic's 6540828583), so the three generations show in no click until a world lays them, and the term that parts the doublets is OPEN; the coupling family (the held spring and the hands' tie, G3) is in the code, with the tie booked on its own edge, and G3's stop and the tie's four-leg residual are OPEN.

**A mathematical miracle.** One law over every derivation that shows one (line 35, item 17; the owner's word, relayed at 00:05 UTC in the Boss's 6536206854, in translation: "I meant this, and what will solve the big problem. Things like this, which are a mathematical miracle, usually fit the solution ... put it into Solution24."). A derivation sometimes shows a result that comes out of the act by itself and unasked, a mathematical miracle (the escapement's W = L^2, with no root, is one); such a result usually fits the solution. When a text that shows one is refused, the refusal does not close its road: a refusal of substance goes to Big Pic first (line 35 item 19), and the problem it names goes back to the deriver to be solved under every law; the road closes only on a theorem that forbids every cure, its hypotheses named. The laws do not bend for a miracle: no root, no fake, no table, the click world and the double check stand over it, and a price only as the night's word allows, named and listed as not closed. A miracle that is a clock fix, the escapement's W = L^2, stands on the board; what it does for physics is read in the click world. A root read by a carry is still a root: that road is closed by the law (Big Pic's 6536836528, his p12 A). The night's hunt for miracles not taken (the owner's order of 00:3x UTC) landed at 01:04 UTC with seventeen candidates, four STRONG and three CLOSED (the Boss's 6536909238; the ledger holds the rows); each a candidate and no result, each with its deriver.

**The board's four and the click world's three.** The board has four dimensions: the three axes of the Node-clocks' lattice and the clock (the 00 slot of the four-vector's square, the ten slots of a 4 x 4). The click world has three: a click stands at a place on the three axes, and time is no axis there, only the order of the clicks and each clock's own count (the owner's request of 00:0x UTC, the Boss's 6536186355 item 3, agreed by Big Pic's 6536221174 item 7). A term DERIVED on board readings keeps DERIVED, since a derivation of the act is a statement about the act; its row names board reading only, and no claim of physics rests on it until its two click-world readings stand. **The conversion from the four to the three** (the owner's word to Big Pic of 06:4x UTC, relayed in his 6540287835, whole in SCRIBE-OWNER-WORDS.md: after the law the decoder converts the board's four dimensions to the click world's three by the conversions known in the world, seen through the clicks): Big Pic's picture (PICTURE.md section 8) gives five conversions, each a reading of click lines with a check that can fail, in whole numbers and no root: C-a time and distance by echo (an observer lays light at its own count t1 and takes the echo at t2: 2t = t1 + t2 and 2r = t2 - t1, the direction the light's step counts along the three axes; it fails if, on a board alike everywhere, two clocks at rest read each other at two distances); C-b the interval (t^2 - r^2 = t1 t2; it fails if two observers read one pair of events at two intervals); C-c between two observers (k the ratio p / q of the counts between two signals taken to the counts between their laying, the dilation (p^2 + q^2) / (2 p q), the speed (p^2 - q^2) / (p^2 + q^2); it fails if k from A to B differs from k from B to A, if three observers on a line break k_AC = k_AB k_BC, or if the twins' free path counts less, D7); C-d two clocks at two depths (the 00 slot's conversion; it fails if k differs, on a still board, from the ratio of their own counts over the same rounds, a board check); C-e lengths (the ii slots' conversion, echo distances along each axis; the reader's note: its "fails if the 24 move them" cannot fail on a board the 24 keep, and the isotropy check that can fail compares an axis with a face diagonal and a body diagonal); no round is read as a duration in any of them (D4). All five read each clock's own count as its own lines in the record's order between two of its meetings (the Boss's ruling on FACE2, 6540720252: nothing new is needed, the face is not D6, and if it is ever carried it is a board field) and wait on light's click lines with the echo's rule; no click writes into the gears, so every observer's times and distances read alike under every path of the third click. **The order** is Big Pic's, with the Boss's yes (6540451346) and his rulings of 6540720252: (1) FACE2's field is closed by the ruling, and the first item owed is a steady clicking clock with light's lines and the echo's rule; (2) light's click lines and the echo's rule as a text (LIGHT2, its verdicts in the register: A's quiet fails because the edge's coupling (750,000) dwarfs its edge key (3), so every move clicks, which is KEY2's question, the key from the structure; on the mesh world the link inside each Node-clock, tied train to wave train, is the board's act and not a meeting, so Big Pic's candidate chain of first clicks stays a candidate; his light/ probes read the medium's own clicks on node-edge 88b4d281c's mesh world, the first click across a Mesh at distance d falling at round 2 d, both ways, at 76 of 76 distances, the far side's peak at round 227 and the bump's return at round 450 (2 x 227 = 454 against 450), a patch of the held hand laid at 2 answering at round 84 and its answer reaching the bump at round 204; these stand as the numbers a lawful light reading must meet once the key comes from the structure, and not as light's lines); (3) a world that lays a clock that moves, for C-c and the twins, after (2); (4) C-a to C-e in the decoder after that, each with its failing check and a pair of hostile readers. The first and the second item also carry seven of the eleven views owed (the teeth W, the grips, the metric's push, the frozen clock, light's echo, mass and the 24's isotropy) and the light reading of two more (mesh.py and the pull).

**Where a fault is sought.** One rule over every mend (line 35, item 18; the owner's words of 00:40 UTC, relayed in the Boss's 6536641902, whole in SCRIBE-OWNER-WORDS.md): the board is clocks that mesh with each other; the click board is the clocks' meetings: each event is one quantum passing from one clock to another, at one place, with no time axis, only the order of the events and each clock's own count, its space read only through an echo of light between clocks. A clock's own working, its gears, teeth, grips and act, is mended and checked on the board; a meeting of clocks, a passing, an exchange, the books and any claim of physics, is mended and checked in the clicks, under the law of Where a claim of physics is checked. Big Pic's reading of the click board (his 6536448148 item 2): the event is a place, the two clocks, the quantum and the order; each clock has its line of its own events; light's links between clocks are the decoder's reading 7, owed; whole quanta cross from the board to the click board as events, a round crosses as order only, a label crosses as a place and never a length, a hand's phase does not cross, conservation crosses as the edges' consistency check at each click (the books, holding by construction). The decoder is built accordingly (the owner's top priority); no status moves by the rule alone; the ban of Where a claim of physics is checked stands for claims of physics, and for a clock fix MEASURED on the board is the full word, since its claim is about the clocks.

**The pace and the blockers.** One rule over the night's work (line 35, item 19; the owner's words, relayed in the Boss's 6537101472, whole in SCRIBE-OWNER-WORDS.md): everything advances only the building of the Node-clock, with as much match to nature as possible through the click world and the board the clocks need to work in; a derivation takes 20 minutes at most; unnecessary tests, blockers and bureaucracy are thrown out; preventing the fakes is the most important. The Boss's application, which the owner can undo in any part: every derivation hands back within 20 minutes and each hostile reader within about 25, blocking items first, the pages short; a refusal goes to Big Pic first only when it is of substance, a claim, a law or a design, and a minor item or a wording goes straight back to its writer or deriver; each test file's room is its count plus a fixed slack of 40 lines; a test goes if it guards none of a law of the act, a file rule, a reading with its mutant, a world or a fake, and the mutant tests and the law's tests stay; work that does not serve the Node-clock's build waits; the gate and the writer's reads stay as they are: the fakes come first.

**The statuses of a term.** Every term and part stands at one of these and moves one step at a time; a status that cannot be shown to stand falls to OPEN. Only the writer enters a status into this document (How we work). The writer sets OPEN where he sees something missing or unclear and tells its owner and the others in the thread at once, so that what is stuck is named until it climbs to READY, ready for code. CHECKED is no step of the ladder: it names a result confirmed in whole numbers by a probe, and it stands only as far as its readers have passed it. A reading of the decoder (section 3) is no term: its column says in plain words built (the code is on main and reads a record made in memory), owed, to mend, or the stage it waits for. A term's row says its status, its stage and one pointer; the readers' items, counts, shas and measures stand in tools/derivations/open_items/SCRIBE-STATUS-LEDGER.md, newest first under the term. A measure's word is MEASURED on the board or MEASURED in the click world (Where a claim of physics is checked, above).

| Status | It says | Who sends it |
| --- | --- | --- |
| OPEN | something is missing or unclear; the line says what, and whose it is | the writer sets it; whoever finds the gap tells him |
| CLOCKED | it works by the clocks: the clock layer is written and holds | the author |
| DRAFTED | the algebra and the physics behind the clicks are written; any local run or check is named (step 8) | the author |
| DERIVED | a hostile mathematician and a hostile physicist both pass it, the last pair new to it (step 9) | the author sends the two passes; the writer enters it |
| READY | derived, and in the Boss's hand: ready for the code writer | the Boss |
| BUILT | the code is written, a hostile reader has read its diff, the writer has approved the candidate as no fake, and main holds it | the Boss, on the writer's approval |

## How we work

The method is Solution24 and the ladder of statuses above; these are the roles that run it and how a piece moves (the owner's words of 2026-10-30, relayed from 12:32 to 14:26 UTC by the stamps of the Boss's posts). Each change of the way of working reaches the writer from the Boss in the same hour, and the writer enters it here.

**The focus.** Until the Node-clock is done it is the one work (the owner's word, relayed at 14:29 UTC in the Boss's 6525206443, in translation: "Now we are focused only on the Node-clock. Only on the Node-clock; we do nothing else until it is finished."): everyone may take it, run it with the runner (`python -I tools/tensor24_run.py`, as examples/README.md has it) or probe it on the real act in whole numbers, and check it locally; code in src/, tests/ and tools/ is written only by the Boss's code writers. What lies outside the Node-clock waits: the Uncertainty lines' re-run, the Koide line, and the decoder beyond its basic part. The aim is a standing Node-clock (the owner's word to the writer, 15:2x UTC, in translation: "Reach a standing Node-clock! And if something does not work in the method, say so."): one that stands and works, to see gravity, the lengthening, the Meshes and the clocks. Its open items are named in section 6, each OPEN with its owner, and where no solution comes from above the item stays OPEN and the Node-clock stands and works all the same (the owner's word, relayed at 14:50 UTC in the Boss's 6525676674, in translation: "If there is no solution from above, leave it among the open things, but write that the Node-clock will stand and be able to work; the rest we solve with time"). Main holds the Node-clock since 00:10 UTC on 2026-10-31 (b1a50bb33, on the owner's word "yes, push now" after the writer's APPROVED) with its five worlds; its free strengths are CHOSEN and named and the act's window is named; main takes each further change by the road. The code writers' branches and node-all's state are in section 6, "The branches".

**The order of work** (the owner's word of 2026-10-31, 10:20 UTC, relayed in the Boss's 6542163897, in translation: "Reach a general picture, closed and structured, that Big Pic runs. Clear from the clocks. Clear tensorially. Clear in the click world, which is the shadow. And then put it into the code. And one clean main."): first the picture, one closed, structured picture run on Big Pic's bench (bigpic-3clicks), each part clear in three views, the clocks (the board's clocks and gears), the tensor (the one algebra) and the click world (the shadow, what the clicks show), what is OPEN in it staying named until it closes; then the code, the writers putting the closed picture into the engine as one generic act; then one clean main. Until the picture is closed and read by its pairs no new physics enters the engine; the settled cleanup goes on, since it prepares the one clean main, and it moves no reading (node-min, node-rec and node-key, RUNNER-CUT and DECODER-CUT, node-fold's fixes). **The method for every open item** (the owner's word of 10:32 UTC, relayed in the Boss's 6542276607, in translation: "As David found how everything follows from the structure, Big Pic is to go on with everything that is open in the same way ... this delicate balance and the miracles are what will bring the full solution ... the clocks, the algebra, and the click world, which is the physics."): derive from the structure the balance each part needs, a condition between numbers of the one algebra and never a value laid; then check that the one algebra meets it; give each part in the three views. The breakthrough (section 6, the bullet of that name) is the first case: the floor comes out by itself when the structure gives two clocks on a two-leg band of strength t each a mass s >= t/2. **The guiding line** (the owner's word of 10:37 UTC, relayed in the Boss's 6542321265, law line 1,976): the freedom is given and everything is fixed in advance, the start and the end are fixed, the clicks choose the path and the board is fixed; the runs must show it in two halves, the board fixed (from the start the board's end is one and the run back reaches the start exactly, MEASURED on every shipped world's run back and a test of the code) and the clicks choosing the path (one start, several click paths, one board end, the run back to the same start), which is OWED until the third click's member runs, as one of the closed picture's runs on Big Pic's bench; no code changes now, and when the third click enters the engine after the picture closes the second half enters the tests beside the first. This document's three views per part (the owner's order of 06:2x UTC) are the picture's frame (section 6, "The picture's frame").

**The roles**

- The owner decides, and a law changes only on his word. Every question for him and every report to him comes to the Boss from every session, none goes to him directly, and where this document says that something goes to the owner it goes through the Boss: the Boss brings him what needs his word, with the reason and a recommendation, and brings his answer back, so that the owner's word reaches the team through the Boss (the owner's word, relayed at 15:53 UTC in the Boss's 6527072352, in translation: "Ask the writer to pass all his questions through you, and not to ask me directly. He asks me in his own session and I do not answer him. All the questions go through you, because you are his manager, not I. Make everyone work this way: they report to you, not to me."). When he speaks to Big Pic, his words stand as Big Pic posts them, and Big Pic's questions back to him go through the Boss.
- The Boss manages everyone, the writer, David, Big Pic and his own agents, and derives and writes nothing himself, neither a derivation nor code, only through his agents: a piece called the Boss's is an agent's draft and passes its two readers like any other. The decoder and the visualizer are the Boss's, their code is the code writer's, and none is built before the Node-clock stands.
- Big Pic looks at everything from above and works with the Boss alone: where a piece is stuck or tangled, or has to be solved from above, Big Pic brings the Boss the solution in a few words. Everything still open in the code is his to solve on his own bench (the owner's word of about 09:05 UTC, relayed in the Boss's 6541517655, in translation: "Everything open in our code, Big Pic should do on his side, as he solved the clicks: he should solve everything open in our code on his side. Everything else they should implement, so that everything is implemented. Only what is missing and unclear, Big Pic should do. Confirm that you understood and sync with Big Pic."), in whole numbers and Rule3's carry, each item with its tests and its books, handed over settled; the code writers implement what is settled and the team only helps him (section 6). His pages and probes are his own files on his bench (tools/derivations/open_items/derive/bigpic, under the gate); src, tests and tools are the code writers' alone. Big Pic also checks every refusal of a reader again, for where the reader is wrong.
- The writer keeps the law, this document and Solution24; he enters into this document what is sent to him, once he has checked that it contradicts nothing, reads every push for faking and approves each candidate before main moves, and says where the method does not work, to the Boss, who brings it to the owner (the owner's word to the writer, 15:2x UTC, in translation: "if something does not work in the method, say so").
- David derives his pieces under the Boss's order and works with the Boss alone.
- The code writer, the Boss's one subagent, writes all code and tests (section 4).
- Hostile readers, a mathematician and a physicist, read every derivation, each new to it at the first reading, the same pair again for the blocking items of a second text and a new pair at the end (step 9), and a hostile reader reads every diff.

**How a piece moves**

- Every question is worked by Solution24. A term climbs the ladder one status at a time; whoever moves it sends the change, through the Boss, with its sha, and only the writer enters a status here.
- A local draft on the real Node-clock is free (step 8). Main takes a term when it is DERIVED and READY, by the road of section 4: the code writer, a hostile reader, the Boss's review, the writer's APPROVED, a fast-forward.
- What waits on another piece runs meanwhile as a local draft on the real act, so that nothing waits in a queue. Every agent and reader writes its findings to a file as it goes, so that an interruption loses nothing.
- A piece that its readers refuse twice, or that waits on a law, goes to Big Pic at once (step 13).
- A text that its pair refuses goes back to the same pair, for the blocking items only, and the Boss's readers resume with their notes; a new pair reads it only at the end, before DERIVED (step 9).
- Every finding, by anyone, goes through the steps again before it is stated (the law that closes the steps, **Before a finding is stated**).
- A deriver, the Boss's agents' and David alike, checks himself before he reports, and his report names what he checked and how (step 8).
- Every NOT PASSED and every REFUSED comes to Big Pic first (the owner's word, relayed at 14:37 UTC in the Boss's 6525393685, in translation: "Even if someone refuses something, do not take it as truth. Always check it again"): Big Pic reads it from above for where the reader is wrong, a claim the text never made, a check that tests something else, a law the reader misreads, a demand beyond the law, and tells the Boss in a few words which items stand and which do not; the Boss's deriver agent folds the ones that stand and answers the others with their reason.

**The thread**

- Every post reads the thread (#2187) to its end first and says what it addressed by Solution24.

## 1. The parts

```
 world        loader           Node-clock        runner           record        decoder       visualizer
 3 files  ->  runner/files, -> engine/,     ->  runner/run,  ->  record/   ->  decoder/  ->  look/
              board, load      algebra/          rounds, rewind   schema
                                   ^                  |
                                   +----- main.py ----+    the one joint between the Node-clock and the runner
```

**The Node-clock is the most important part** (the owner's word of 2026-10-30, 11:2x UTC): every other part lays it, runs it or reads it, and it is told first below.

| Part | What it is | Home | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| World | three files of whole numbers: the universe, the piece, the setting | examples/tensor24-click/, examples/tensor24-mesh/ | BUILT |
| Loader | the Node-clock's initialization: lays the clocks from the three files | runner/files.py, board.py, load.py | BUILT |
| **Node-clock** | the one act at every Node-clock, every round: the core | engine/, algebra/carry.py | BUILT at a standing W (section 6); terms 5, 6, 7, 9, 11, 12 and 13 DERIVED, term 10 OPEN (ONELAW3's form named and not built; G2 to G5) |
| Runner | the rounds, counted from outside, and the run back | runner/run.py, rounds.py, rewind.py | BUILT |
| Record | the boundary between the board and the clicks | record/schema.py | BUILT at schema 1.3; schema 2.0 on node-fold (its version a label by the schema's own major rule, the old engine's nine records cut) and 3.0 on node-rec (the teeth out of the record); schema 1.4 on node-edge (the click line's sides and across, the header's dial and birth, the end's teeth) |
| Decoder | reads the record's clicks against what nature shows (the Boss's) | decoder/ | built: the click world's block of seven readings (C5, C6), each SHOWN, or NOT SHOWN, or NOT READ, or NO CLICK, with its cause; the record's reading and the basic part stand; on node-third also the sides guard (decoder/sides.py) and on node-third-readings the three clicks' readings (decoder/three.py) |

## 2. How they work together: one round

The loader lays the board from the three files. The runner gives every Node-clock its turn in every round: each one reads itself and its six Meshes at the now slot, steps every gear once through Rule3's carry, and writes only its own state; nothing is queued or sent between Node-clocks, and the order of work is no law. A click, a train's own click tooth carrying its swept area to a whole key (on node-edge each edge side's pair of teeth carrying the passing between two clocks), is term 11's, DERIVED for the pair at one Node-clock: the runner writes it as a click line of schema 1.3 (1.4 on node-edge, with the line's sides), one per round, Node-clock and train whose click tooth carried (on node-edge every clock joined to a side that carried, with every edge side of its clock listed). The runner writes the record and the decoder reads it. The run back is, round by round, the act on the mirrored state, mirrored (n rounds by n unticks), and returns the start bit for bit while every fine tooth stands within its teeth (on node-edge the edge teeth run back from the end line's teeth, and restarted at their birth they miss); under a moving W one mirror about the n rounds does not return the start (section 6). main.py passes the Node-clock's functions to the runner, so the two never import each other.

## 3. The parts

### The Node-clock (the core)

The engine is five modules (engine/): step.py, mesh.py, contract.py, kinds.py and state.py.

- **Clock:** a clock of many hands, one gear per row. Every round every gear moves once: its six neighbours pull it through the Meshes and the coupling pushes it; it turns by what its teeth carry and keeps the rest as its fine tooth; the drive carries the coupling's push the same way. No gear waits, sends or is singled out. A Node-clock is independent: it reads itself and its six Meshes and nothing else (line 92), and there is no special code and no law of its own for a slowing, a grip, a sending, a receiving, a lengthening, a shortening or a freezing: these are what the act gives on the metric's own gears (law, line 90), once the metric's push is built (G2).
- **Algebra:** the act on the edge between a gear's two slots (the half-open convention, the owner's decision): D_in = W(b) + W(a); N = 2 (D_in (a - b) + 2 (s F + K)) + s; q, s+ = split(N, 2 D_out), D_out = W(a) + W(a+), n = q 2D + s with -D <= s < D by one carry from the dial's half; a+ = a + q; b+ = a. F is the sum over the six Spokes of grip (neighbour - own); K = s T_k q_d, with q_d, r_d+ = carry(J, T_d, r_d). J = J1 + J5: J1 is the plan's contraction of the readings through the 24's tables on the now slot, each monomial of up to four legs on node-live (any number of legs on node-onelaw) pushing all its legs, with the Mesh plan; J5 is the metric's push (term 5, the books' form), every slot pushed by minus the derivative of the act's own energy by it. Each train's click tooth carries the passing of the round's window, the before, the now and the next: S = X(a, b) + X(a+, a), X over the train's pairs on the value legs' plan on the Node-clock's own counts, and q_c, k+ = carry(S, key, k); on node-edge the passing is per edge side, P(t, u) the given less the taken, one carry per side at the edge key, so that q_tu + q_ut = 0 by the carry's law. The mirror is (b, a, -1 - s) on an even row and (-b, -a, s) on an odd one, the click tooth kept, and the run back is the tick on the mirrored state, mirrored, exact where every tooth stands within its edge's dial and the push reads the momenta of no row it lands on (hypothesis (d)). The plans read the tables (the coupling family by character, the push, the notch, the held hand's spring, the ties and the gradient vertex).
- **Board:** one pass at every Node-clock alike, reading only its own gears and its six Meshes at the now slot and, for term 11's passing, its own counts alone at the before slot and at the count the round produces, no push kept from a round to the next (built in main at d6ec46ff0, the value legs). The teeth W are the rest plus the clocks' read of the square family's 00 slot, k0 c00 with k0 = Spokes x senses as the loader lays it (on node-onelaw the clocks' Plan, monomials of any number of legs read on the counts given first), the 00 row's own teeth at rest; each Spoke's grip is the rest plus the ii slots at both ends of its Mesh; the drive's teeth T_d are senses on the square's 00 and ii rows, where the push lands, and 1 on the force's path. The passing reads the value legs' plan alone, so neither the Mesh plan nor the metric's push enters any train's X: the Mesh flux is booked by the Mesh's edges on node-edge (plans.crossing, 88b4d281c) and the clocks' exchange with the metric by no click this night (NOT_CLOSED 4 and 31). The run back is, round by round, the act on the mirrored state, mirrored (n rounds by n unticks).
- **Clicks:** the record holds a click line (round, site, train, q signed, the tooth before and after) per train and Node-clock whose click tooth carried in a round, a give below 0 and a take above it (schema 1.3, a9f8a440a; schema 1.4 on node-edge adds the sides and the sides across a Mesh). A whole click is the carry of a train's swept area by its own tooth (on node-edge per edge side, the line's sides [u, q, tooth before, tooth after]); the split click is the passing split by the structure over a triangle's three edges, and the third click the triangle's own tooth landing one circulation (the law of the three clicks). The clicks read the rest beat as a mass, and a clock's own count as its proper time; the board's wave alone fixes the metric only up to a scale until term 6's spring, so proper time and lengths are not separable before it.
- **Home:** engine/step.py, engine/mesh.py, engine/contract.py, engine/kinds.py, engine/state.py (the five modules of the engine), algebra/carry.py, main.py. **Test:** test_the_tensor24_engine.py (J; the act against its naive model on the plan and the Mesh plan, local, pure and turned by the 24, at a standing W (section 6); and on the edge, the click tooth carrying a pair's passing with the mirror keeping it, the metric pushed in the books' form on the push's readings, the 24 turning the act alike under the twisted action, and the run back where every tooth stands within its edge's dial and the push reads no row it lands on; each part's yes and no as plain assertions, each case named in its comment: the code writer's cut leaves one small test per part, the owner's word of 12:4x), frame.py (the property tests any engine of the Node-clock must pass, shared by the tests: locality, purity, rename, the 24's images, the run back), test_the_tensor24_algebra.py, test_the_tensor24_run.py (the whole once, on our bang); test_the_tensor24_edges.py (term 11 on the edges and the Mesh's edges), test_the_tensor24_half.py and test_the_tensor24_lifts.py (the 48 lifts and the doublets), the last three on node-all. **Status:** by term, below.
- **On the branches beyond node-live, named by module (the architect's list L):** term 11 on the edges (node-edge f71c19390 and 50af24bde: runner/plans.py edging, the edge form of engine/step.py passing, the per-side teeth in engine/state.py, runner/run.py joined, meeting and teeth_of, schema 1.4); the Mesh's edges (node-edge 88b4d281c: plans.crossing, step.crossing, run.py crossed, schema 1.4's across, tests/test_the_tensor24_edges.py); ONELAW3's (b), the widened given-first step (node-onelaw 08c66fd40: engine/step.py weight on the clocks' Plan), (c), the k-leg monomials (f494b0aa6: engine/contract.py pushes, engine/state.py Plan(legs, factor, target)) and (d), the units' machinery (e871dfb75: main.py typed; load.scaled is cut from src by the Boss's ruling); node-half's pieces (0893f2f28: engine/kinds.py lifts, Halves, Lifts.axes, on_cosets, hamilton and turned; runner/files.py spun and halved_departures; runner/load.py kept_forms; runner/plans.py selves and widening; tests/test_the_tensor24_half.py and tests/test_the_tensor24_lifts.py); the decoder's sides guard and three clicks' readings (decoder/sides.py and decoder/three.py with their tests); Big Pic's mode 11, the triangle's own tooth, is a probe beside the act on bigpic-3clicks and no src.

**The square family** (a train of the Node-clock, no row apart; read as the metric in the click world's physics)

- **Clock:** the clock's own train: its 00 slot is read into every gear's teeth here, its ii slots are each Mesh's grip; it is found by its kinds' character.
- **Algebra:** the four-vector's symmetric square, a scalar, the vector, a scalar, E and the shears (the 00, 0i, ii and jk slots; xx, yy and zz carry the scalar and E), with W = rest + the 00 slots, linear (decision 41).
- **Board:** it rides the act like every train; its push from the clocks' rows is term 5's, laid by plans.pushing in the books' form (B2b df4fa644c).
- **Clicks:** a deeper 00 slot is a slower clock, read by its clicks as proper time; gravity reads massless.
- **Status:** the reads BUILT; its push DERIVED at k0 = 12 (term 5, the fifth text), built in main as a frame (B2b, df4fa644c); the 00 row's own law is open (section 6).

**The thirteen terms** of the Node-clock's act

**The mass** (term 6; David's, with G3's results)

- **Clock:** a hairspring ties a hand to a held hand in its own clock, so it swings by itself.
- **Algebra:** the spring g C n, 4 sin^2 (omega0 / 2) = g n / W.
- **Board:** a tied hand beats at its rest rate at every clock alike; the board wave's hands have no hairspring and only travel from clock to clock.
- **Clicks:** one quantum's beat is read as its mass, through energy differences and through motion; a swing of many quanta reads their interaction; the board's wave reads massless.
- **Home:** engine/contract.py, term 6. **Status:** the vertex law BUILT and tested generically; the spring's beat and bound were checked at the tests' strengths and left the suite (git 9143d7092); the held rows' own spring is laid in the click and mesh worlds' files at 10^8 (writer D, 36fbfbbde); G3 is DERIVED by both end readers and the strengths follow Big Pic's rule.
- **G3's line, DERIVED:** David's page DAVID-TENSOR24-G3.md is DERIVED by both end readers at its fifteenth text (toys-5 3bbe5ab73, notes folded at ff921b0ed): the beat is K / W throughout, the pump's wave is near a quarter of the size's beat, a second tongue sits near the size's own beat at larger swings, the tied train is the unit spring until the hand's tooth steps, and the window is scoped to the R measured with its lower bound OPEN.

**The families and the three hands (the three generations)** (drawn by Big Pic from the decoder's page 1.6, 1.8 and 3.9 to 3.11, git 8c4f3ff59; David confirmed it with one mend, 6522837453)

- **Clock:** a family of kind T1 is a clock with three hands, one per axis. The cube's 24 turns swap them as they swap the axes, and the act drives all three alike: the same teeth, the same grip, the same wave. A held count beside them is a hairspring. Of kind A1 it ties all three alike, so one beat; of kind A2 it ties none, and only couples a T1 family to a T2 one. Three beats need a held count that points along the cube: E off its three lines, or T2 off the body diagonals, with an A1 part large enough that no hand runs away.
- **Algebra:** the 24's kinds have 1, 1, 2, 3 and 3 rows, and T1 x T1 -> A1 is the dot product. Three beats need held counts that cut the 24 to a group under which T1 has only one-dimensional pieces (D2 or the Klein group fix the basis; a half turn or nothing lets the counts set it). A1 + E (n0; e1, e2) gives n0 + e1, n0 - e1 + e2 and n0 - e2; T2 gives lambda^3 - |t|^2 lambda - 2 t1 t2 t3. Any three beats fit a + B cos (phi + 2 pi k / 3), so that form predicts nothing alone. Koide's 2/3 is a test, David's (board2mass 7.2; OPEN: its probe m2 left the tree on the owner's word of 12:5x and is held in git, scribe-5 6183738b1, so it stands as no DERIVED or CHECKED until a whole-number probe and a new pair of readers stand; David takes it after G5 and G2): never met on the diagonal A1 + E spring with whole counts (the Eisenstein norm's power of 2), met often with a T2 part. That too is a board reading (the owner's word above): **its click-world reading is Koide's ratio from the three species' own clocks**, each species at rest beating at its own rate, its mass read as its face's rate between two light links; 2/3 asks for the masses' square roots, which no face holds, so the reading is the whole-number form, the condition with the roots squared out, the product of its four sign branches, a polynomial of degree four in the three beats, written by the decoder's deriver, the branch with every root positive the one read, read twice in two worlds of one design; it waits on a world that lays the three species (Big Pic's 6537282655). At most three alike, the board's axes, under four assumptions: a natural three, not a derived one.
- **Board:** at every Node-clock the three rows step alike; with a held E or T2 count each row beats at its own rate. That count must not show the cube's axes to the board's wave: the kinds that split a triplet are the metric's own (E, T2), so a vertex pairing them with the metric's slots makes the board's wave's speed depend on direction. That obstacle, and nature's bound of parts in 10^18 that cavities set on it, are board readings set against nature and close no road (the owner's word to Big Pic, relayed in his 6537282655: "The obstacle for them, you checked it on the board, and you need to check it in the click world."; line 35 (16) and (17)). **Its click-world reading is a Michelson reading** (Big Pic's 6537282655): an echo alone cannot show it, since an echo defines the distance and so makes light's two-way speed the same in every direction; the ruler must not be light, the same count of places, which crosses as a count; one clock sends light along two directions to two mirrors that count of places away and its face counts each round trip, the axes showing if the two counts differ; it is read twice, by a second clock and with the whole set turned by one of the 24, each time with the splitting count laid and without it, and it waits on light clicking with matter at the clock and at the mirrors (NOT_CLOSED 4: no world writes light's click lines), on the faces (FACE) and on the decoder's echo reading (C6). An E count's own potential sits on its lines, where two beats meet, wherever its term in f to the first power is not 0 (a three-leg or five-leg term, or a cross term with the count's A1 part); from six legs on, f^2 and every higher power of f can move it off.
- **Clicks:** a click takes the whole train and never a row, so unsplit the three hands are one species' gears: the law's confinement (line 1556), the colours as the three axes. Split, they are a candidate for three species alike but for mass, told apart only by a take resonant with one beat; then the rows are the generations and the colours stand elsewhere, since rows cannot be both. No click shows them so far: light writes no click line in any world (NOT_CLOSED 4) and no world lays three species, so "the three generations that appear in the clicks" (the owner's word to Big Pic, 6537222053) is the goal and no reading. In nature the electron, the muon and the tau are one clock design at three beats: one charge and one spin, told apart by the beat (the mass), the life (the width) and the neutrino each pairs with, each heavier one decaying into lighter ones, all three taken alone. The quarks' generations are gears, read only through their hadrons, and the top through its own decay.
- **Home:** none of its own: term 6's spring, and G5's take. **Status:** A1 or A2 held counts give one mass (read once on g3_strengths (a) and (d) and g3_kinds_and_parts (e) to (g) at toys-5 c7b0a4bea and found correct by the fifth text's two readers, but those probes broke the law: a lay through series sines, two roots found by bisection and a printed sine formula with typed results; they left toys-5 at 2927b2e63 under the owner's word "Sine is forbidden" and leave main, their record stays in git at c7b0a4bea, and the result is no CHECKED until a probe without them repeats it, G3's ninth text resting on g3_hat.py and g3_pump.py; DERIVED when G3's page agrees), which nature refuses as the generations' law. - **The quaternion's road to three generations** (Big Pic's 6537793603; the owner's two words to him of 01:5x UTC, whole in SCRIBE-OWNER-WORDS.md; OPEN: the algebra checked exactly, the physics owed to the click world, no world lays it): the 24 whole units are three eights, Q8 and its two cosets by omega = (-1 + i + j + k) / 2, whose turn carries i to k, k to j and j to i; under them a half-speed hand on the three axes splits into exactly three doublets, alike in all but omega's charge, 1, omega or omega^2, and the 24 units modulo sign are A4, the flavour group of Ma and Rajasekaran (2001); the three eights are the weights of Spin(8)'s vector and two spinors (triality as the reason for three generations is a speculation in the literature, no result). Any coupling that keeps omega makes the generations its three Fourier modes, each landing on each axis with share 1/3, with masses a + 2|b| cos(phi + 2 pi k / 3), a form any three numbers take, so it predicts nothing alone. **GEN3** (HALF2's deriver; DERIVED on the board): the share per axis row is exactly 1/3 for each doublet, and a real spring never parts 2' from 2'', since every real coupling commutes with omega's charge Q; parting them needs b's phase, which a quarter turn and the run back each flip. The click world's held hand turns exactly that term (its charge sigma flips under the quarter turns and the run back and holds under the 12 turns that never swap two axes), and under line 102 the coupling must come from the families' law, not be typed. **The parting term** must be odd in Q and act on the rates, sigma Q (a - b), and two things stand in its way: the run back (a rate leg breaks the push's reading of the now slot, and a reversible version needs a matrix division, not Rule3's carry) and where it would come from (sigma is time-odd and the families' tables lay value legs only). Big Pic's turning frame (6538128481) keeps the run back or the 24, not both (the text GEN5 was NOT PASSED by both); the power road is refused three ways (counts past the width, a loop in the act, a cosine); his counter-turn road (6538416837) is OPEN; the parting term stays OPEN, its lawful form asked of Big Pic. ### The world: the three files

- **Clock:** before the first round, the files say which trains every Node-clock carries (each a name, its kinds among the 24's five, and on node-half among the 48's eight (five whole and three half-speed), its part and its pinion, which the loader checks equal to den and no act reads), the universe's three integers (den, full_rate and width, from which every tooth and every grip is laid) and the strengths the worlds' files still name, whole numbers (key, push, notch, hold, spring, tie and mesh), which the strengths rule replaces (no strength is chosen: a coupling is its table's own entry, every scale the initialization's), how many Node-clocks stand on each axis, which gears are set where, and how many rounds the run makes (the setting's key ticks).
- **Algebra:** whole numbers only; every side, part and integer is at least 1, a strength at least 0, the push 0 or 1 and the key odd; no fourth file is a world.
- **Board:** at round 0 every gear stands at 0 except the ones the setting lays. Nothing else is laid: no coupling, particle or mass is declared (decision 35), and no strength is chosen (the owner's word, "Choosing a strength is forbidden", law line 102; the strengths rule, the Boss's ruling of 04:24 UTC): the integers the universe file still names are listed in section 6 with the law that should give each (the night's scope, section 6).
- **Clicks:** none come from the files; everything the clicks show comes out of the act (a mass is measured only there, decision 37).
- **Home:** examples/tensor24-click/ and examples/tensor24-mesh/ (universe.json, piece.json, setting.json each), the shipped worlds, each laid by a generator kept in the repo (derive/boss/), with examples/README.md; on main three more folders of the same three files ship, the bang, tensor24-live and tensor24-well, which node-rec takes out, our bang being then a test's lay of the same three files, frame.strengthless in tests/frame.py, written by the test that runs it. **Test:** test_the_tensor24_files.py (its yes walks every example folder) and test_the_tensor24_worlds.py (the click, live, mesh and well worlds, on node-rec the click and mesh worlds, each run their first rounds out and back by the run tool's own function, to the digest the run wrote in tests/tensor24_prefixes.jsonl, never typed; its no a setting changed by one count). **Status:** BUILT; the universe's chosen key must be odd, the format refusing an even key by name (484fb8d72) and any push other than 0 or 1 (996df846f).

### The loader, the Node-clock's initialization

- **Clock:** it makes every Node-clock alike from its star of six Spokes: the 24 turns that leave the star the same, the five kinds by which a train's gears turn with them, and the tables that gear one kind into another. Then it sets every gear's teeth and every Mesh's grip, and lays the setting's gears.
- **Algebra:** the 24 are the matrices of three Spokes that Rule3's weight keeps (24 of 216). A channel's group sum is c u u^T, and its table u is the primitive row, read where the diagonal entry divides every other, with the sign by Rule3's sign test; all 125 channels go in one einsum, with no loop, seed or search.
- **Board:** each Node-clock's six neighbours, by the wrap on each axis. Every row's teeth rest at 6 den Gamma^2 (6 is the Spokes, Gamma the universe's full_rate), and every Mesh's grip at den Gamma^2. The square family is found by its kinds' character alone: its 00 slot is every row's teeth, read at k0 = Spokes x senses = 12 (B2b, df4fa644c), its ii slots are each axis's grips. The held hand is found by its character too (the one-dimensional kinds together, A1 + A2, at part 1) and its rows hold no Mesh's grip, a site-local oscillator, the tied trains by their character kept under the sign kind's twist, the board's wave and the square untied (B2, c853dee00). s and T_k stay at 1 until G3, and T_d too but on the square's 00 and ii rows, whose dial is senses (B2b, the push in the books' form).
- **Clicks:** the record's header names each train's kinds and part, so a click names a train, never a row. No number of the loader reaches the clicks except through the act.
- **Home:** runner/files.py (reads the files and refuses by name), runner/board.py, runner/load.py, runner/plans.py (the plans' home: the pairs of rows, the spread, the push's and the notch's monomials and the click's passing, and the held hand's spring, the ties and the gradient vertex: rows_of, holding, tying, joined and meshing; on node-edge edging and crossing, the edges between trains and across the Meshes; on node-half selves and widening), engine/kinds.py (the 24 and the kinds, and on node-half the 48 lifts, which main.py's made joins to load.tables), main.py (made); tools/tensor24_files.py (checks and loads the files). **Test:** test_the_tensor24_files.py, test_the_tensor24_load.py (which holds the 08:1x ruling: an edge by the structure alone and a chosen 0 its factor, with its yes and its no), test_the_tensor24_kinds.py; the engine's and the run's tests run it through the act. **Status:** BUILT; the strengths follow Big Pic's rule (no strength is chosen; the integers the files name wait to be laid by it); its values (the rest 6 den Gamma^2, the grip den Gamma^2, s = T_k = 1, T_d = 1 but on the square's 00 and ii rows) are pinned by the load test from the files' integers (main 9e3c14ff8, after the Boss's audit found a rest of 7 den Gamma^2 passing every test); hold, tie, mesh and push are CHOSEN in the universe file, hold, tie, mesh and push at 0 in the base and the live worlds (c853dee00) and push 1 with the notch 120000 in the well world (B2b; the base, live and well worlds are out on node-rec), and hold 1, tie 96,000 and the key 50,811,429 with push 0 in the click world (B3), and mesh 250,000 with hold 1 and tie 0 in the mesh world (B5; chosen again at 6d9887945, since at 200,000 the tied hand never moves once the pulse is laid at rest), and the held rows' own spring CHOSEN (writer D, 36fbfbbde): 0 where no held train stands and 10^8, the grip at rest den Gamma^2, in the click and mesh worlds (a third of W, inside its window 0 < 2 spring < 4 W, on no exact turn); the key must be odd (two teeth born at (key - 1) div 2 sum to key - 1 only for an odd key, which keeps the pair's books: the code reader's M6 and the second reader's finding 3), the push is 0 or 1 (term 5 claims no strength: the second reader's finding 2), and the passing gives the square the factor 0 by its flag, as the push does (M1; 484fb8d72).

### The runner

- **Clock:** it counts the rounds from outside, since no Node-clock holds a count. It gives every Node-clock its turn in every round, then runs all the rounds back to see the start return.
- **Algebra:** the act iterated; the start returns bit for bit.
- **Board:** before each round it reads the promise, from outside: both slots' teeth at least 1 (a tooth below 1 named as a tooth), every tooth within its edge's dial, -D <= s < D with D = W(b) + W(a), the wave's window (the Spokes' grips summed within [0, W] per sense) and the width (every count of both slots within the universe's width, a runaway named at the round where its counts first pass it), and names each departure where it stands, the run and its tool writing nothing then. After the rounds it rewinds by the act's run back alone. The end's digest is the state reached.
- **Clicks:** it writes what the act gives: the record's header and end, the click lines of schema 1.3 (one per round, Node-clock and train whose click tooth carried, a9f8a440a; on node-edge schema 1.4, a line for every clock joined to a side that carried, with the line's sides, the header's dial and birth and the end's teeth) and the ledger beside them.
- **Home:** runner/run.py, runner/rounds.py, runner/rewind.py; tools/tensor24_run.py. **Test:** test_the_tensor24_run.py. **Status:** BUILT. **The owner's order** (his word to the keeper directly, about 09:4x UTC, in Hebrew, in the keeper's translation, whole: "The runner feels huge; it must be shortened and be generic and clear..") is in force as **RUNNER-CUT**, an OPEN item of this part (the Boss's ruling of 6541888909). Its form: three files in, the one act in rounds, the record out; no function per term, one builder serving every coupling and reading its clocks and edges from the files in place of plans.py's builder per coupling, and one call in load.py in place of one per plan; what node-rec and node-min take out (the silent worlds, the record's teeth, the unit rows, plans.edging) counts toward it. Its check can fail: every shipped world's record equal line for line before and after, except what node-rec takes out on purpose; the pinned full check passing; a code reader reading it; the keeper counting it. Its target: the runner no larger than the Node-clock it runs, by the keeper's count on the same head (the runner's folder is 1,011 active lines on node-fold against the Node-clock's 338), clarity first and no line packed to meet it, the writer naming what stands in the way if it cannot be met clearly. Its writer: a fresh writer, from the merge of node-rec and node-min once both stand on clean pinned checks. Not in it: ALG2's cochain act, which changes the act and enters the engine only once ALG3 settles it with its pair and Big Pic rules it. No bound is held by the gate: the owner answered "No." (09:52 UTC) to the Boss's question on a gate bound for the runner and "Yes." (09:56) to the Boss's reading of it (the Boss's 6542001729), so the target is the writer's and the keeper counts at each push. Its preparation is the first step of writer J (a counter by the keeper's rule, a comparer of every world's output line for line, and the plan), and the cut itself runs on node-short, from the merge of node-rec, node-min, node-key and node-fold's fixes.
