### The record

- **Clock:** the boundary between the board and the clicks; only the readers read it.
- **Algebra:** whole numbers of any width, and labels.
- **Board:** nothing of the board crosses it except what its lines name. The header: the record's name and version, the engine, the files with their digests, the board's sides and the order of its sites, the kinds with their dimensions, each train's kinds, part and winding, the integers' width, and two fields no reader reads, the runner's name and the operands, still written (schema 1.3 retired the couplings, the classes and the draw); schema 1.4 (node-edge) adds the dial D and the birth of every edge side's teeth to the header. The end: the rounds, the rounds run back, the digest, and whether the run back was exact; on schema 1.4 every edge side's teeth. The ledger beside it: one count line per round, Node-clock and train (1,280 on the example).
- **Clicks:** its click lines name a train, a Node-clock and a round, and on schema 1.4 their sides and the sides across a Mesh, which orders the clicks and is never a duration; nothing is computed in it.
- **Home:** record/schema.py. **Test:** test_the_tensor24_run.py and the decoder's tests. **Status:** BUILT at schema 1.3 (laid in main at b10d7d008), schema 2.0 on node-fold (its version a label, as the schema's own major-version rule decides; the old engine's nine records and their reader cut) and schema 3.0 to come on node-rec (the teeth out of the record: lines and sides with q alone, the Boss's 6541741909): a click line {round, site, train, q signed, tooth: [k, k+]} in place of the Whole lines, each kind's characters and each train's key and square flag in the header, the couplings, the classes and the draw retired, the decoder reading the new line; the runner writes it at a9f8a440a (the night's plan, B4): one click line per round, Node-clock and train whose click tooth carried, the forward run's lines alone; schema 1.4 on node-edge (f71c19390, 50af24bde, 88b4d281c): the click line's sides [u, q, tooth before, tooth after] and across [u, place, q, tooth before, tooth after], the header's dial and birth, the end's teeth and across, one ADDED entry (MINOR 4), and ACROSS, the across field's list, on node-third-readings.

### The decoder (the Boss's)

- **Clock:** the watchmaker outside the case, reading the click board, the clocks' meetings (line 35 item (18)): each event is one quantum passing from one clock to another clock, at one place, with no time axis, only the order of the events and each clock's own count, and space only through an echo of light between clocks. It reads only what clocks count and how one clock's click meets another's: a reading is a clock's own count between two of its clicks, never a round read as a duration and never a label's difference read as a length. Clock parts first: a click takes a whole train and never one of its gears, so a gear is read only through its clock's clicks, and only a clock is placed in spacetime.
- **Algebra:** read a line and refuse a bad one by name; group, order by round, follow a link (a take to its lay, a clock to its clicks); count, add, subtract and multiply whole counts; compare fractions by cross products; Rule3's quotient with its remainder. Never a root, a float, a logarithm, a circular function, a physics formula or a number beyond 0 and 1 in the code; a spread for finite draws is an integer of the files.
- **Board:** the board reaches it only through the record and the ledger beside it, read from outside; it never steps the engine, never reads its arrays and never writes into a run. The step from the board to the clicks is the delicate one: the board is no fixed space (a Node-clock keeps its label while the Mesh between two labels lengthens and shortens), its round, teeth and grips are no observables, they reach the clicks only as ratios at first order and as departures of order omega0^2 beyond, and the board holds no unit for the round.
- **Clicks:** nature is read in the clicks, the board's shadow (line 35 item (16)): the edges' consistency check at every click (the books: a code check that says HOLDS or FAILS and never SHOWN, since a verdict that cannot fail is a fake), the meetings, each clock's line of meetings, proper time, the cone, the twins, light's echo, masses as rest beats, Born's shares and Bell's correlations, each a count of clicks (the readings below). Every other reading says SHOWN, or NOT SHOWN with its first counterexample named, or NOT READ with its cause named, or NO CLICK where the record holds no click line ("the record holds no click line: not in the click world"), and raises nothing.
- **Home:** decoder/ (it imports the record and the algebra alone: records.py reads the record by the schema's guards, clicks.py lists and counts the click lines, measure.py holds the verdict, the Meshes and the cone, shadow.py reads the click world, sides.py is the sides guard, on node-all; three.py, the three clicks' readings, is on node-third-readings) and tools/tensor24_decode.py (on node-third-readings with --readings, --twin, --apart and --swap). **Test:** test_the_tensor24_decoder.py (the record, the ledger, the tool, the click lines listed and counted), test_the_tensor24_decoder_measure.py (the Meshes and the past cone) and test_the_tensor24_decoder_meetings.py (the click world's readings, each with its yes, its no and the mutant it catches); test_the_tensor24_decoder_sides.py (the sides guard, on node-all) and test_the_tensor24_decoder_three.py (the three clicks' readings, on node-third-readings). **Status:** built on records made in memory and on the five worlds' records (C5 49e97c992, C6 22a12a328, the cut 8069c3630; the owner's build order of 23:51 UTC, a basic decoder on the clicks that shows the physics there): the tool prints the click world's block after the basic listing, seven readings: the books at each click, the meetings, each clock's line of meetings, a duration as a clock's own count (D6), the twins (D7), the cone check by click (D8) and light's echo (reading 7). On the click world laid on the CHOSEN spring (writer D, 36fbfbbde) the held train clicks and the books, the meetings and each clock's line read NOT SHOWN (Big Pic's read of 02:49 UTC: the held pair's charge, the step's Casoratian, holds at -1 at the exact quarter turn and at a generic spring moves from -1 through 0 to +1 within 300 rounds, after which the held train clicks alone; the held hand's square climbs through the run, so no runaway holds only for the 5,000 rounds read; the run names a runaway where counts pass the universe's width, writing nothing, node-live a66674061); the mesh world reads the same way on main and SHOWN on node-all (7,605 of 7,605 places); since writer B's re-lay (node-live 5ecb100f5) the click world is laid with the held hand at rest, its run naming a count past the universe's width at round 3,752 and writing no record. In the three worlds with no click line (the bang, the live world and the well; on node-rec a test's lay of our bang) every reading reads NO CLICK with the refusal line last; D6 and D7 read NOT READ (the act holds no clock's own count as a field, and no two clocks meet twice by two paths; a clock's own count is its own lines in the record's order, the Boss's ruling of 6540720252), D8 reads NOT READ where no line could stand outside the lay's cone, and light's echo reads NOT READ (LIGHT2's verdicts are in the register). The readers of lines no run writes were cut with their tests on the owner's word that unnecessary tests go (decoder/books.py and bangs.py deleted, 702 test lines out); the click readings of G5's split clicks are written with G5. **The owner's order** (his word to the keeper directly, about 09:5x UTC, in Hebrew, in the keeper's translation, whole: "The decoder also feels too long.."): the decoder is to be shortened; decoder/ holds 756 active lines on main (records.py 267, measure.py 187, books.py 166, bangs.py 99, clicks.py 37) and 605 on node-fold (records.py 254, shadow.py 205, sides.py 92, clicks.py 39, measure.py 15), its tool 41 and 60 and its test files 1,018 and 617; it is in force as **DECODER-CUT** (the Boss's ruling of 6542001729), with the runner's: its form, one guard by the schema's own fields and the readings from q alone after node-rec; its check, every world's printout equal line for line except the ruled changes; its target, no larger than the Node-clock it decodes, by the keeper's count (605 active lines against 338 on node-fold), a new reading entering only within the target or with a reading cut of its size; no bound is held by the gate (the owner's "No."); its writer, K, after node-rec.

**What it reads.** The record's header (the files, with the setting file's text, the sides and the order of the sites, the kinds with their characters, the trains with their kinds, parts, windings, labels and each train's square flag), its click lines of schema 1.3 (a round, a Node-clock, a train, q signed, the click tooth before and after) and, on node-edge, schema 1.4's sides and sides across a Mesh, read against the train cards and refused by name where one names a train no card names, holds q 0, a tooth below 0 or not below its card's key, or stands twice at one round, site and train (C4, 9ccbc6481), and its end (the rounds, the digest, the rounds run back, exact or not); the ledger's count lines, one per round, Node-clock and train, the train's counts there row by row. **Uncertainty**

- **Clock:** one tick holds every beat, and only many ticks tell a beat; one Node-clock holds every motion, and a sharp motion fills the whole board.
- **Algebra:** a wave's support times its transform's support is at least the board's size (Donoho and Stark), each motion read as held or not by its turn-sum's exact remainder on the cyclotomic polynomial.
- **Board:** the Node-clocks a wave holds times the motions it holds are never fewer than the board's Node-clocks, a count with no law of its own (whole numbers with no draw: every wave of -1, 0 and 1 on rings of 2 to 10, and every wave of a class on three boards, the example's among them; reader-5 f35b4b1cb).
- **Clicks:** a click at one round holds every beat, so a mass is read only through many clicks of one clock; the draw puts a click's place in the detector's region, and within it the motion is open.
- **Status:** DRAFTED: the whole-number re-run's second text (reader-5 f35b4b1cb) names the bound as Fourier's support bound on the board, leaves what makes it Heisenberg's to the clicks, and recounts the waves at the bound by Smith's characterization, the same in every class; the first text's readers agreed with changes (six and six blocking items, all folded) and the second text's readers agreed with changes too (1 and 4 blocking items, all sentences around the bound; reader-5 987c62e67), and it waits outside the Node-clock; the first probe left the tree and stands in git (scribe-5 6183738b1). **The owner's law** (his word on Q11, relayed at 09:09 UTC in the Boss's 6541517655, in translation: "By law, the click world must work by the uncertainty principle. It must come from the clicks."): the principle stands in the law already (**The great clock is deterministic, but it cannot be read through clicks, because of the uncertainty principle**; **The uncertainty principle is the hand's and the click's**), and the click world must work by it, derived from the clicks and not set beside them: UNC1's relations are the carry's band, a board check, and are not that; two click observables read from lines alone, place by the echo and motion by Bondi's k, are OWED, and the derivation is Big Pic's, second in his order of work. The owner's word of 09:30 UTC on the teeth, "Throw them out of there.", puts them out of the record entirely: every reading of the clicks reads q alone, which already holds in the decoder (the Boss's 6541741909), and the checks that read teeth read the board's own state. The owner's question of 10:10 UTC, whether the uncertainty is in the click world because the third click does not come alone, like the third generation, is routed to Big Pic's bench as an input, his third item: ALG3 (0e308f17, PASSED by both readers) measured the one integer link between the lines and the circulation: a third-click landing w adds c w to the sides, moves no line (B c = 0) and moves the circulation by 3w (c . c = 3), DERIVED and MEASURED exhaustively on one triangle (6,561 of 6,561), so the mod 3 residue is the first click's own and the third click is invisible to every line, even mod 3, and never comes alone into any line (the owner's lead of 10:10 UTC holds in its strong form); that is an exact, total hiddenness and not a trade-off, the lines' part and the circulations' part forming a direct sum with both sharp at once, so "complementarity" is the wrong word, and whether it becomes an uncertainty relation is OPEN, Big Pic's (the Boss's 6542188661 and 6542202933).

### The algebra (algebra/carry.py)

- **Clock:** a dial turns, and what it cannot hold stays at the Node-clock as its remainder.
- **Algebra:** n + r = q d + r' with 0 <= r' < d: the one place a division is written; the operations are three, on whole numbers: addition, multiplication and the carry; the module holds carry, flip (the mirror's w - 1 - r) and lit (the zero test, by one carry); no float, no root, no exponential (lines 100 and 102).
- **Board:** n + r = q d + r' at every gear, every round: the quotient leaves as the event, the remainder stays as the memory that makes the run back exact.
- **Clicks:** the quotient is the click; nothing else is measured.
- **Home:** algebra/carry.py. **Test:** test_the_tensor24_algebra.py. **Status:** BUILT.

### The visualizer (the Boss's)

- **Clock:** the board's clocks drawn as they turn.
- **Algebra:** n/a: it computes nothing.
- **Board:** the Node-clocks, the Meshes and the rounds as the record has them; its board side can draw the count lines now.
- **Clicks:** the clicks drawn where they land, with the decoder's verdicts beside them.
- **Home:** tools/look/tensor24_page.py with its template tools/look/tensor24_page.html (on node-fold, node-view c9c2fda8b merged at 86201c4d8). **Test:** test_the_tensor24_page.py. **Status:** built on node-fold, the owner's word of about 06:50 UTC (the worlds seen running; the text VIEW1): the tool draws one run's record, and the ledger beside it where one is named, as one self-contained page of two views, the board with its clocks and the click world with its meetings in their order, with the decoder's own readings and verdicts beside them, computing no reading and no physics and showing no light or matter on the board; a record of no click line is drawn as a page that says so; the ninth code reader reads node-fold with it. **A named departure** (the Boss's 6542001729): the page tool (tools/look/tensor24_page.py and its html) holds numbers beyond 0 and 1 (six in the .py, its byte bound 15,000,000 among them) and the operators "//" and "%", and lies outside the auditor's census and the gate's reading; it is a drawing tool, forbidden to grow, its owner the Boss's writer.

### The guards, beside the run

- **Clock:** the law's clause "The system is a system of clocks, and it is always checked" (line 106): the guards read every push and every line.
- **Algebra:** the gate (tools/derivations/open_items/scribe_scan.py) is the algebra's law in a script: it refuses a push that raises a count of a number beyond 0 and 1, a float, a root, a loop, a branch, a comparison, a clamp, a mask, an enumeration, a probe with a float, or a module whose docstring lacks these four layers, and it holds the table of section 5.
- **Board:** the tests run on a board: each generic part has one small test and the whole runs once on our bang (line 106), the standard of the world and no more: no test is defined for nothing, no matrix of cases, no table of mutants (the owner's word of 12:4x UTC); tools/check.py runs the gates and the tests together.
- **Clicks:** the decoder's rows are the validation: what the clicks read is set against what nature shows.
- **Home:** docs/ALGEBRA.md; tools/check.py (runs the gates and the tests together, in CI on every pull request and on every push to main), tools/merge_base.py, tools/record_code_shape.py, tools/tests_shape.py, tools/documents_gates.py, tools/derivations/open_items/scribe_scan.py (the gate; its self-test beside it, scribe_scan_selftest.py, runs with it), tools/derivations/open_items/scribe_law_check.py (the law's own check and this document's mirror of the tree, `--arch`). **Status:** BUILT; the law check, the mirror and their self-test are to join tools/check.py's ratchets (the Boss's yes, 12:4x UTC; check.py does not run them yet, and they wait on the code writer's held commit 293f01412); the tests are cut to that shape by the Boss's code writer alone, the engine's and the decoder's (the decoder's to three), its first task (the owner's words of 12:4x and 13:2x UTC).

## 4. How to write code

The code writer reads Solution24 and this section first at every task, as its standing instruction. Each line is a law that refuses code, with its line of docs/ALGEBRA.md; the gate's rules and what holds each stand in tools/derivations/open_items/SCRIBE-GATE-1.md.

**Who writes, and how a change goes in**

- The Boss's code writer, one subagent, alone writes the system's code and tests, the decoder's included (the roles: How we work); the writer keeps these documents and the gate (the owner's words of 2026-10-30, 12:3x to 12:5x UTC).
- Every derivation, the Boss's agents' included, passes its hostile mathematician and its hostile physicist first, each new to it at the first reading and a new pair at the end (Solution24, step 9); only then does the Boss brief the code writer for main (the owner's word of 2026-10-30, 13:0x UTC), and no status of DERIVED or CHECKED stands on one reader alone.
- A change is written in its own worktree, the full check against main exits 0, a hostile reader reads the diff, the Boss reviews it and pushes the candidate to tensor24-review, the writer reads that sha for faking and posts the approval, and only then main moves, a fast-forward with no force (12:4x).
- Locally anything may be built and checked; main takes a change only when its derivation stands in this document as DERIVED and READY and its candidate is approved; no code for main starts on what is not READY (12:5x).
- The hard pieces (a term's code, the act, the metric's push, the clicks, anything a derivation lands or an exact run back hangs on) go on the strongest model, the rest on the default (12:5x).
- No old code, no old test, no old world returns; the old engine stands in git at the tag v4.0.0 (11:0x).
- Nothing weakens the gate: no change of tools/derivations/open_items/scribe_*, of tools/check.py's ratchets, of tools/documents_gates.py or of the workflows travels with a code change; they are the writer's.

**The code writer's commits, check and reader**

- One commit per step, in its own worktree, from main's head; the first line says what changed and why, and the session's two attribution lines come last; never uv.lock, never --no-verify, never a rewrite of main's history; it never pushes, the Boss pushes.
- Before every report: `python tools/check.py --full` with CHECK_BASE=origin/main exits 0, and the gate (tools/derivations/open_items/scribe_scan.py, its lists read from origin/scribe-5) refuses nothing.
- The hostile reader is a fresh reader, new to the change, who reads the diff against the laws and this section and looks for: a test bent to pass or a test of nothing; a number behind a name or a count typed; a gate or a list edited; an if, a loop or a mask in the Node-clock; code the document does not name; a docstring off the four layers. Each finding comes with its file and line, and the code writer folds every finding before the candidate.
- It never touches: the gate's files and lists (blob for blob from scribe-5); docs/ALGEBRA.md and docs/ARCHITECTURE.md (it sends the writer a mend through the Boss); .github/ and uv.lock; tools/tests_shape.py's budgets, except to lower them (and, on the owner's word of 2026-10-31, 01:2x UTC, to set each at its file's count plus a fixed slack of 40 lines: line 35 item 19).

**The laws in code**

- Whole numbers, no float anywhere (line 100): no float, true division, round or float call, no math, random or fractions import; every integer within 128 bits (line 50).
- No number beyond 0 and 1 in the code, none behind a name, an enumeration, a table or a length (lines 96, 100): the numbers stand in the three world files.
- No circularity that makes a phenomenon happen, no root, no cosine (line 102).
- Rule3's carry is the one division, with its remainder kept: no other floor division, remainder, power or shift (line 46).
- The Node-clock has no if (lines 35, 96): no branch, comparison, clamp, mask, choose or floor; a whether is the carry's quotient.
- No loop in the act: the machine is one tensor operation (line 35).
- Locality is never broken (line 92): a Node-clock reads itself and its six Meshes and nothing else; no global, no cache, no log read in the act; state lives in the Node-clock alone.
- No books, no special function or key for a link, a horizon, a sending or a receiving (lines 102, 90).
- Nothing is declared (lines 35, 106): the files name every family, train and coupling, and the code reads each role from them, never by a name or a place.
- The cube's 24 turns hold (line 94), and information does not vanish: every act is undone bit for bit (lines 31, 35).
- A probe under tools/derivations is whole numbers too (line 100).
- Each part imports only what section 5 allows.

**What a fake is** (the owner's own definition: his words of 2026-10-30, 10:2x UTC to the Boss, "All the worlds but our bangs are fake; we don't need them", and 11:0x to 11:2x UTC to the keeper, "There are no numbers hidden behind enums and such, right, because that is a fake"; of 2026-10-31, 00:1x UTC, "First confirm that the writer and the current code have no fakes, that numbers exist only in the files we approved, and that there is no cosine, no sines, no formulas; that the files are clean, that there is no fake in the files. ... Enums that hide things"; and of 07:0x UTC, directly to the keeper, to define it here; each whole in SCRIBE-OWNER-WORDS.md): **a fake is anything in the code, its tests, its files, its readings or these documents by which a result, a verdict or a status stands that the act, run from the three files alone, does not itself give: put in under another name, or standing where nothing could refute it.** The act is one pass of Rule3's carry over the whole numbers the files lay; what it gives it gives with its remainder kept, and it runs back bit for bit; whatever else makes a result appear is a fake. Its forms, each with what catches it (the gate's rules are those of tools/derivations/open_items/scribe_scan.py, listed by `scribe_scan.py --rules`, and the keeper's independent scan of src/tensor24 reads the same forms again from the syntax tree):
1. **A number behind a name:** a number that is neither in the three files nor 0 or 1, however spelt: a literal, a constant's name, an enumeration, the labels of a tuple, the length of a list or a display, a table, a chained slice, 1 + 1 for 2, a count in disguise (law lines 96 and 100: no number in the code). Caught by the gate's rules NUMBER (a number beyond 0 and 1 in the code, counted per file), ENUM (an enumeration), TABLE (a typed table of integers), LENGTH (a count spelt as a length), TWICE (a two spelt as a sum) and TWOSLICE (a two spelt as a slice); a count typed in a test or a reading is caught by the readers.
2. **A verdict that cannot fail:** a reading whose check holds whatever the record says (a typed verdict, an identity read as a test, a quantity compared with itself, a reading with no refusal of its own), and a test that passes whatever the code does or that no mutant of the code breaks. A reading counts only where a changed record or a changed line of code makes it read NOT SHOWN: the decoder builds each verdict from two readings, and each reading has its yes, its no and the mutant it catches (the decoder's tests, section 3). Caught by the readers, and by the gate's note PIN (an assert against an integer beyond 0 and 1 in a test, counted per file).
3. **A formula or a value standing for a result:** a circular function, a root, an exponential, a logarithm, pi, a float or a true division; a number of nature typed in a probe or a reading; an expected result typed in beside what the act gives (law lines 100 and 102: whole numbers only, and no circularity that makes a phenomenon happen). Caught, in the code, by the gate's rules FLOAT (a float, a true division, round() or pow()), IMPORT (math, random, fractions, decimal, statistics, scipy, sympy), DTYPE (a numpy constructor with no dtype), CIRCLE (a circular function or pi), ROOT (a call of an integer root) and MIX (a rotation or a hypercomplex product spelt as a tuple of bilinear sums); in a probe under tools/derivations/, by PROBE (a float, a true division, a root, a circular call or an import of the modules above), SEARCH (a circular function or a root made by hand) and the note SINEWORD (a word that names a circular function).
4. **A value found by search or picked for an outcome:** a root by bracket, doubling or Newton, a range searched until a phenomenon shows, a strength, a key, a window or a lay chosen so that a run stays inside it or a turn lands exactly; choosing a strength is forbidden (law line 102; Big Pic's 6538866082: the law holds no strength, every coupling its table's own whole entry at 1, every scale and the key the initialization's, so that a world is its lay alone and a runaway or a still hand is the lay's reading and never a reason to re-pick). Caught by the gate's rules SEARCH (a series summed by a loop, a bracket halved by its midpoint, a search that counts up) and CHOOSE (a call of choose or nonneg in the act or the loader), by FILEVALUE (a number that a world's file holds, typed again in a probe) and PELL (a pair of whole numbers typed in a probe that solves a Pell equation), and by the readers, who read every number an act reads back to the structure or the initialization.
5. **A decision in the act:** an if, a branch, a comparison, a clamp, a mask, a sign test or a choose in the Node-clock, or a loop in the act; the act decides by the carry's quotient alone (law lines 96 and 106: the Node-clock has no if). Caught by the gate's rules BRANCH (an if, a conditional expression or a match), COMPARE (an ordering comparison), MASK (a selection by a condition), CLAMP (min, max, abs, clip or where), LOOP (a loop or a comprehension in an engine module), REFUSE (a raise in an engine module), OPERATION (a power, a shift, an exclusive or, a floor division or a remainder outside the carry's home) and ALLOW (an operation off the closed list of addition, multiplication and the carry), and by the note INDICATOR (a quotient spelt as a mask or a cap, a whether over the structure), each such note read by the keeper one by one.
6. **A channel the Node-clock does not hold:** state outside the Node-clock, a global, a cache, a log read in the act, a read beyond the six Meshes, a thing named by its place or its name where the files should lay it (locality, law line 92; no train is declared, law line 324). Caught by the gate's rules STATE (a module-level container, a global, an id() key), MEMO (a module-level cache), LOGREAD (a read of the great clock's log), BOOKS (a name that keeps accounts beside the state), SPECIAL (a name for a link's lengthening, a horizon or a clock's freezing), POSITION (a name indexed by a constant integer), FAMILY (a family named by a string in the act or the loader), DECLARE (a family, a train or a coupling named in a file or in a world's code) and ISOLATE (an import across the engine's folders).
7. **A world the files do not lay alone:** a world built in code, a synthetic world, a lay tuned to a result, or a world kept beside the shipped ones that nothing reads. Caught by the gate's rule DECLARE for a declaration in code, and by the keeper's reading of every world's files at each head: the shipped worlds are the files' lays and nothing else.
8. **A status that no proof carries:** DERIVED on one reader or none, a reading called SHOWN that the decoder does not show from the record, a statement of physics on the board where only the click world's reading may stand, a part called BUILT that its branch does not hold, a part this document does not name, a trace of the old engine. Every status of this document names its proof (the text and both readers); where there is none the part says OPEN or OWED. Caught by the documents' six gates (tools/documents_gates.py), the law check (tools/derivations/open_items/scribe_law_check.py) and the keeper's reading of every push for a status.
9. **A test or a gate bent:** a bound loosened, a tolerance widened, a case skipped, a budget raised, or a gate or a list edited in the same change as code that needed it; nothing weakens the gate. Caught by the gate's own rule that a push may not raise any rule's count above the merge base's or the ceiling's, by the budgets of tools/tests_shape.py, which may only be lowered, and by the keeper's reading of every diff that touches the gate's files and lists.
**What is not a fake:** a departure that stands named, with its reason and its owner, on this document's lists (a strength a world file still names; a FRAME; an OPEN item; a view OWED); it is a named open item and is forbidden to grow. The keeper's verdict words are four: Fake (any form above, unnamed or unfalsifiable), Forbidden (a construction the law forbids by name, present), Named-open (named, with its reason and its owner) and Lawful (with its reason). A fake is a finding for the owner the hour it is found; the keeper reads every push for the forms above (the gate, the diff, the independent scan), and no candidate is approved with one.

**The four layers of a docstring** (line 35): every module of the engine, the algebra, the runner and the record, and main.py, has lines that start Clock:, Algebra:, Board: and Clicks:, each with text, and n/a where a layer does not apply (the decoder has six modules: clicks, shadow, sides and three carry the four layers, and records.py and measure.py are owed them; the packages' __init__.py files hold a line); a module stays under 400 lines.

**The tests** (line 106): one small test per generic part and the whole once on our bang, the standard of the world and no more: no matrix of cases, no table of mutants, no test of a particular, none for a part with no code yet; a test is named in its part's Test line.

**The document and the tree mirror each other:** a module, a test or a tool that no part of section 3 names is refused (`scribe_law_check.py --arch .`, which reads no `__init__.py`: the readings package, whose only file is one, stands named by no part), and a change of a part's code changes its Home, Test and Status lines in the same push.

## Derived from the structure

The law is the structure, and a number the structure gives is no law line: it is a derived result, entered here with its derivation and its standing (the Boss's ruling of 11:3x UTC, his 6542866096 items 4 and 5, on the owner's words to David, "Maybe it's exactly half then..?" and "Why a law when the number follows from the structure?"; the law's lines 1,974, 352, 358 and 436 are struck for it and stand in git at f858843d4). **The owner's order**, whole in SCRIBE-OWNER-WORDS.md and carried by the law of the strengths (line 1,970): at 11:47 UTC, "And the structure, that the numbers come out of the structure: try to do it on the whole board. And then in fact we are left with no numbers at all on the board."; at 11:5x UTC, "All the numbers in the system from the structure... from a mathematical miracle and from the algebra of the springs and the whole numbers, everywhere.." The target is every number in the system, everywhere (the Boss's 6543062093, withdrawing his own 11:47 reading that set the lay apart). **The three routes** are the owner's words: a mathematical miracle (an identity exact in whole numbers, such as the leapfrog kept exact by Rule3's carry or the triangle's 2 : 1 : 1), the algebra of the springs (the square form and its cells) and the whole numbers (the 24, Rule3's carry and the Gaussian ring). **A standing** is one of three: derived and passed by its pair; a candidate (derived as algebra or measured, no pair having read it, or the owner not having approved it); laid and OPEN. Big Pic's one algebra owns the routes, each closing goes to a pair through the Boss, and a writer takes a number out of the files only when its closing is settled (spring is the first).

**1. Derived results**, each with its statement, its derivation and its standing.

- **The floor** (the law's former line 1,974; the Boss's wording of 12:5x UTC, 6543685420 item 2, which replaces his 12:4x wording after FLOOR1c's pair and is entered as a derived result): "The floor comes out of the structure's own masses and bands: DERIVED given the masses; that the structure gives them rests on the square form, a candidate. On one band with equal springs (the click world's giver and taker), exactly half the band gives the edge, and more than half holds the form. On any structure with equal springs and equal bands the edge is at -lambda_min(A) t/2. Under the square form a connected group holds by its springs alone exactly when some loop is frustrated; otherwise it sits at the edge with one free swing. Under the two laid ties a held hand's swing lends that swing c h^2, and holds it only while it lends, not for good; under David's cell it lends it nothing, and the swing is free at its laid step. On the board the run's hold is MEASURED on its lays and windows, within the step's law." *Why "the form":* a wide held hand fells the board's run even at s = t (MEASURED: held lays from 90 up fall at lambda 100), so the hold above half is the form's, DERIVED, and the board's run is MEASURED. *Why one band with equal springs:* "half" is the pair's number; on a ring of four, s = 3t/4 gives no floor at all. *Derivation:* David's, from the pair's quadratic form (FLOOR1b and its successors, toys-5): for two clocks joined by a band t each of a mass s, the form (s + c h^2) I + (t/2) X, X the swap, is non-negative at every lend exactly when s >= t/2 and the run is held by the masses alone exactly when s > t/2 (unequal masses: s_1 x s_2 >= and > (t/2)^2, both positive); under Big Pic's square form each mass is half the sum of its bands, so a single band gives the edge and an odd loop, a triangle first, gives more (Harary's balance of a signed graph). *Standing:* the algebra and the measured rows are confirmed by both readers of David's own pair; FLOOR1 was refused by both pairs on one point (exactly half is the edge and not a hold); FLOOR1b's pair was NOT PASSED (12:4x UTC); **FLOOR1c is PASSED by the ALG pair** (12:5x UTC, the Boss's item 1: the physicist no BLOCKING item and six MINOR, 37 of 37 comparable lines byte for byte; ## 7. The documents

Two documents tell the system: this one and the law ([docs/ALGEBRA.md](ALGEBRA.md)). [README.md](../README.md) is the front door (`llms.txt` its machine copy), [CONTRIBUTING.md](../CONTRIBUTING.md) the way in, [CHANGELOG.md](../CHANGELOG.md) what changed, and `LICENSE`, `CITATION.cff` and `pyproject.toml` the project's files. papers/ holds the two publications, their text and PDF (their scripts stand in git). The records of the work stand under tools/derivations (the derivations, the gate's `SCRIBE-GATE-1.md`, the keeper's `SCRIBE-LAW-AUDIT-2.md`, `SCRIBE-OWNER-WORDS.md` and `SCRIBE-ROUTINE.md`), and no part depends on them. The earlier documents (ENGINE, BIG_PICTURE, CLOCK_WORDS, FURTHER_RESEARCH, FOLLOW_UP_PAPERS, HIGHLIGHTS and UNIVERSE24) left the tree on 2026-10-30, the owner's word: one clean main, the architecture file and the algebra file; what of them is live stands here or in the law, and the rest in git at scribe-5 3b7fd1bd2. The decisions in force are the law's (line 35). The old engine and its worlds stand in git's history alone.

**Who keeps what:** the roles, who sends and who enters are told once, in How we work, after Solution24.

## 8. The glossary

- **Node-clock:** a site of the board: a set of gears, one for each row of each train, and one act per turn. **Gear:** a dial with its count now and before (the state's two slots) and its fine tooth, a remainder. **Train:** a tensor of gears across the board, the dials of one kind of thing (the board's wave, the held and tied hands, the square family).
- **Spoke, Mesh:** a Spoke is one of a Node-clock's six directions; a Mesh is the link through a Spoke between two neighbouring Node-clocks, read once from both ends.
- **Rule3, carry:** Rule3 is the one division, n + r = q d + r' with 0 <= r' < d: a quotient that leaves and a remainder that stays; the carry is the only place a division is written. **Winding:** the whole turn of a hand, the divisor at which a carry completes.
- **Click:** the carry's quotient where a winding is whole: the one measurement, of three kinds (the whole, the split, the third). **Round:** one pass of the runner over every Node-clock, an index from outside and no time of the board's. **Now, before:** a gear's two counts, no clock time. **Key:** a whole click's winding, the swept area at which a click tooth carries (a train's own tooth, and on node-edge each edge side's pair of teeth; term 11): the clicks' h-bar, a setting of the board. **Held count:** a train's hand that stands wound at the count n at every Node-clock; term 6's hairspring ties the other hands to it.
- **Mirrored state:** time's reversal at fixed teeth: now and before swapped and each fine tooth r flipped to W - 1 - r in its teeth W; the run back is the tick on the mirrored state, mirrored.
- **Square family, 00 slot, ii slots:** the clock's own train, named "square" in every world file on node-fold (the four-vector's square) and read as the metric in the click world's physics: its 00 slot is every gear's teeth here (the clock's rate), its three ii slots are each axis's Mesh grip.
- **The 24, the kinds:** the cube's turns that leave a Node-clock's star the same, and the five kinds that tie a train's gears. **Board:** the event board, taken in our event space, which has no time. **Bang:** the one run from the three files.
- **Code writer:** the Boss's one subagent that writes the system's code and tests (the owner's words of 2026-10-30, 12:3x UTC). **Approval:** the writer's reading of a candidate sha for faking, posted before main moves (12:4x). **Solution24:** the method of solving, in the section after the introduction. **DERIVED, OPEN:** a derivation that a hostile mathematician and a hostile physicist both pass, the last pair new to it (step 9), entered here by the writer, is DERIVED; until then it is OPEN, CLOCKED or DRAFTED by how far it has come, and no code starts on it; READY says the Boss may brief the code writer (the ladder is under Solution24). **PASSED:** a text or a range of code that a hostile reader or pair passed. **NOT PASSED:** one that it refused. **OWED:** a view or a part that no text gives, with what is missing named. **FRAME:** a form kept for what is not sure, which never raises a status. **CHOSEN:** a strength a universe file names, forbidden to grow. **BUILT:** on node-live, or on the branch the cell names. **SHOWN, or NOT SHOWN, or NOT READ, or NO CLICK:** the decoder's verdicts on a record. **Fake:** a result, a verdict or a status that the act does not itself give, put in under another name or standing where nothing could refute it (section 4, what a fake is).
- **G2 to G5:** the stages that close what the Node-clock lacks (the Boss's plan): G2 the metric's push, the notch and the frozen clock; G3 the strengths and the plan; G4 the shift and the shears; G5 the clicks; G6 the composites that click (the decoder's). **Postulate 6:** the time gear's law, slowdowns composing by their product.
